# Evolution history

## Iteration 1

Configuration: `iteration-001/team_config.md`.
The `joint_program` role investigated shared joint-surface representations and
returned a successful role report. Its rank-32 basis established subset-level
reconstruction evidence; full-domain capacity and query cost remain open.
Independent complete-program team score was not recorded.

Evidence: `iteration-001/team_result.json`,
`iteration-001/joint-program-report.md`, `iteration-001/TEAM_OUTPUT.md`.

## Iteration 2

Configuration: `iteration-002/team_config.md`.
The team assigned parallel work to `landmark_formula` and `normalized_shape`.
`landmark_formula` completed a trainer/preparation report; `normalized_shape`
reached its timeout and left partial artifacts for review. Complete symbolic
training and candidate integration remain unverified.
Independent complete-program team score was not recorded.

Evidence: `iteration-002/team_result.json`,
`iteration-002/landmark-formula-report.md`, `iteration-002/TEAM_OUTPUT.md`.

## Measurement protocol

Subsequent team evaluations use the competition composite formula on two actual
complete 1B runs of one frozen candidate. Mean CPU, mean sampled Acc and two-run
full-output consistency determine the score. Historical sample and 100M results
retain their original scope. The harness appends each new configuration,
independent score and evidence path here.

## Iteration 3 / exp853-eat003-system

2026-10-29T19:26:57.674934+00:00

REFLECT

Only two prior teams exist. Iteration 001 showed rank-32 reconstruction
ability on 5,120 observed joint surfaces, while its direct full-domain
coefficient table exceeded the ELF budget. The tested uncentered codebook
lost accuracy. Iteration 002 prepared a landmark trainer but did not execute
the search; normalized_shape timed out with partial code and no result.
Neither team produced an independently scored complete-program candidate.
These results constrain their particular domains, representations and
resource allocations. They establish neither a ceiling for the task nor
whole-program improvements. Exp825 remains the measured control, and its
current two-run 1B protocol still needs a measured baseline.

The information-flow gap is between subset fit, full-domain deployment and
joint CPU/accuracy evaluation. Earlier reports suggested further work
without an accountable integration phase. Already inspected slab and v0
examples are development data. Keep v1 prediction holdout closed.

EVOLVE

Use two independent discovery roles with different evidence scopes, then a
single integration owner. One starts from the complete executable and
measures mandatory work, including initialization, parsing, prediction,
memory access and output. The other audits unfinished representation
evidence, examines the concurrent symbolic result and establishes the
domain/resource implications of one bounded hypothesis. Each must deliver
an executable, small artifact and the exact boundary of any failure.
The final role reconciles both with the score and accepts only supported
changes. It owns candidate integration and cross-team learning. This
separates independent discovery from acceptance while keeping the final
decision explicitly about the complete program.

## Iteration 5 / exp853-eat005-stability

2026-10-29T20:19:46.674348+00:00

REFLECT

Iteration 1 established useful subset reconstruction but its direct coefficient
storage exceeded the ELF budget. Iteration 2 delivered a trainer without a
completed search. Iteration 3 failed before model output due to a now-fixed
provider configuration bug; its l7 search remains unobservable.
Iteration 4 passed batch32 through 100M output parity and resource checks.
Its independently scored 1B CPU values were 151.835975 and 177.461369 seconds,
with identical full output and 98.881628718% sampled Acc. The mean score was
97.733230708. This is a measured research stage; final Acc >99% is still open.
The current-protocol exp825 1B control has not been measured.

The evidence gap now includes repeated execution conditions and the path from
bounded representation quality to complete-domain implementation. A single
100M pair does not establish stable 1B improvement. Previous rank-16 failure
constrains the specific representation, split and optimization budget.
Its interaction claims need a precise independent audit, and the old PySR
timestamp/current-state statement needs correction while preserving raw evidence.

EVOLVE

Assign an execution-variability researcher and a symbolic-domain researcher in
parallel, followed by an integration and evidence owner. The first must request
a bounded paired measurement early, distinguish known observations from causes,
and discover any implementation improvement from evidence. The second makes
symbolic work executable through the reviewed pipeline and audits deployment
coverage. The final owner reconciles CPU mean, variation, Acc and resources.
The Supervisor runs all r9 measurements serially and reviews search requests.

## Review 5 / exp853-eat005-stability

Retain the batch32 implementation at commit baa2d6f; its dual-1B score is 97.697594421 and its paired 100M timing gain reproduces across both balanced blocks. Four total batch32 1B observations show a 151.835975–178.353845 s range, without a causal explanation. The local-first fallback executed the reviewed PySR search after l7 timed out; its v0 export passed parity on 48,624 rows and a post-selection development audit passed its preregistered paired bootstrap gate. Treat that as a promising research artifact only; no complete-program integration or new Acc claim exists.

Next action: Finish the already-running frozen exp825 dual-1B control under the shared lock and compare its per-run CPU/user/sys/wall and source identity against the four batch32 observations. Then evolve EAT006 around private integration and a fresh 100M screen of the verified symbolic export, retain v1 closed, and continue accuracy work toward >99%.

## Iteration 6 / exp853-eat006-pysr-stability

2026-10-29T21:45:36.263917+00:00

REFLECT

EAT005 completed its evaluator but both discovery roles reached their
12-minute timeouts. They left useful findings: the exp825 1B control used
166.314646 and 184.522259 CPU seconds; batch32 has four observations at
151.835975, 177.461369, 159.496208 and 178.353845 seconds. These are small,
unbalanced samples and do not establish a cause. The matching complete output
SHA, input, evaluator, CPU8 and scoring assets establish comparable program
behavior, while per-run machine state was not captured.

The balanced 100M sequence found lower CPU for batch32 and a larger descriptive
CV: exp825 n=4, CV=0.189%; batch32 n=4, CV=0.698%. This does not explain the
1B spread. The verified v0 PySR export is development evidence only; it has not
been integrated or scored in the complete program. The new mixed archives need
their generation, uniqueness, coverage and exact-label provenance audited
before they inform search selection. Keep v1 predictions closed.

EVOLVE

Increase the previous 12-minute role limit to 60 minutes and define a
three-stage information flow. First, run CPU stability diagnosis and archive
audit independently. Next, let the PySR specialist prepare a frozen hypothesis
and search only on audited development data. Finally, the integration owner
consumes the audit and verified export, screens a private complete candidate,
and updates cross-team learning only with supported evidence. Supervisor owns
search launch, remote-first preflight, r9 measurement, and all final decisions.

## Iteration 7 / exp853-eat007-mixed-v0-run

2026-10-29T23:21:10.854412+00:00

REFLECT

EAT005's prior v0 symbolic model improved a physical-response representation,
but its selection base was far below the complete-program candidate. EAT006
then searched for a sparse correction to the complete-program prediction.
Its chosen formula retained the baseline and passed C++ parity, yet did not
clear the pre-registered selection or audit gains. This rejects the frozen
feature, target and search configuration, not PySR or the complete-program
research direction.

The v0 archive has 100,000,000 unique reachable U-role requests, full 59,904
directed role-pair coverage, and 126/126 sampled labels matched the independent
exact solver across 63 strata. Its generation source and all unsampled label
validity remain unknown. The existing 48,624-row feature sample uses archive
labels that have not all been independently checked. A mutable request created
by the interrupted EAT006 PySR role is unreviewed and must be revalidated
against frozen identities before reuse.

Target-scale timing evidence shows a slower second run in each of three prior
1B invocations, including increases in both user and system CPU. Those
observations have no per-run machine-state trace and do not establish a cause.
The balanced 1B stability request prepared in EAT006 has not been executed.

EVOLVE

Use two independent phase-1 roles. One preregisters and audits up to 10,000 new
v0 labels with the project exact solver, excluding previously audited rows.
The other compares the frozen EAT006 formula against its true `steps_y`
baseline, analyzes grouped errors and identifies a distinct, testable search
direction. Phase 2 consolidates both reports and may prepare one frozen PySR
request using explicit label provenance, pair-disjoint splits, integer-Acc
metrics, uncertainty and deployment gates. It must mark the search blocked if
the verified data volume or expected gain cannot support the registered
decision. It must not start PySR.

Phase 3 reviews only the Supervisor-launched export for the current study.
Candidate changes remain private until the formula passes its training,
selection, development-audit, paired-uncertainty and C++-parity gates, then a
serialized 100M screen passes. The harness's automatic dual-1B run remains
research evidence for the unchanged candidate when no replacement is eligible;
those two runs are a separate pair and are not represented as the preregistered
balanced exp825-versus-batch32 stability sequence.

## Iteration 8 / exp853-eat008-joint-optimization

2026-10-29T23:50:23.871022+00:00

REFLECT

Iteration 4 showed that `EXP596_BATCH=32` improved r9 100M CPU against batch
8 at unchanged sampled accuracy and complete output, and the same change lost
on the local AMD cycle test. The local result excludes that batch-width
implementation on that host; it does not exclude other system-level designs.
The r9 profile attributed 34.16% of sampled user cycles to the `records779`
prefetch site and another 14.00% to its prediction consumer. Reducing random
table access cost and retaining accuracy remains a system-level opportunity.

Iteration 5 scored the batch32 candidate at 97.697594421 on two full 1B runs.
Its CPU observations ranged from 151.835975 to 178.353845 seconds, with
identical output SHA and 98.881628718% sampled Acc. The paired 100M sequence
reproduced a batch32 speed advantage, while its lower variation did not explain
the 1B run spread.

Iteration 6's verified PySR export had zero changed C++ reference rows. Its
selection gain against the same-run complete-program `steps_y` baseline was
0.004656 pp versus a 0.05 pp gate; its pair-disjoint audit change was
-0.000223 pp versus a +0.03 pp gate, and no paired-bootstrap gate passed.
The top-level export gain used the physical-offset baseline. These results
reject the frozen EAT006 target, feature set and search configuration for
promotion. They leave other symbolic targets and representations open.

The EAT006 r9 records share the same 1B input, exact-label sample, evaluator,
output SHA and frozen source identities. One batch32 two-run result and two
exp825 two-run results are valid individual measurements. The second exp825
invocation reused the same warmup study ID, so the preregistered balanced
sequence is incomplete; retain all three records as descriptive evidence and
do not continue the old run IDs. The cleanup warning for the first exp825 run
has been resolved by verifying its remote scratch directory is absent.
An existing low-load r9 observer remains active under the operator's SSH
session; preserve it and record it as host context if another target-scale run
occurs.

Iteration 7 was stopped before role results. Do not resume it or reuse its
study ID. Its planned 10,000-label audit exceeded the 60-minute role budget:
the measured solver rate projects about 60 minutes of solving before the
archive scan. A new 4,032-row stratified v0 audit is projected at about
24 minutes of solving plus about 9 minutes of scanning, leaving room for
validation and reporting.

EVOLVE

Use three phases with five explicit roles. Phase 1 separates archive-label
evidence from complete-program error and timing diagnosis. Phase 2 develops
one private system candidate and one materially different PySR request from
the phase-1 evidence. Phase 3 reconciles both paths and advances at most one
candidate through Supervisor-owned screening. This adds a direct complete-
program systems path beside PySR while keeping source ownership and r9
serialization explicit.

The PySR role prepares, freezes and validates a request but does not launch it.
The Supervisor launches a reviewed configuration through `pipeline.py
launch`, which checks l7 first and uses the local `/home/lhjysyx/EDA2026/pysr_workspace`
when l7 or its inputs are unavailable. The search is capped at 1,800 seconds
of configured training and 2,400 seconds total job time. Search outputs return
to this study's pipeline directory. The candidate role works from verified
exports and screens only a complete private candidate with preregistered
paired accuracy and CPU gates.

## Review 8 / exp853-eat008-joint-optimization

EAT008 base batch32 scored 97.693868325 on two complete 1B runs. The private batch-wide dependent surface-prefetch candidate passed its frozen serialized r9 100M gate with identical output and Acc, then scored 97.822381996 on two 1B runs; output SHA, resources and cleanup passed. Commit c1e2b03 changes only src/worker.cpp. This is a +0.128513671 research-stage score difference on previously observed 1B requests, not an unknown-input or platform result. CPU CVs are descriptive at n=2 and do not establish a stability cause. The forced-only PySR formula failed its frozen accuracy/interaction gates and was not integrated.

Next action: Start EAT009 from committed c1e2b03/exp825 lineage. ## Iteration 13 / exp853-eat013-accuracy-breakthrough

2026-10-30T09:23:37.516347+00:00

REFLECT

EAT008's accepted change improved CPU while preserving outputs. EAT010 and
EAT011 did not change algorithm accuracy. EAT012 finally executed the real
900-second PySR search, but its selected expression gained only0.0007075244pp
on48318 internal selection rows and failed its frozen gates. No authentic
C++ export or complete-program accuracy candidate resulted.

The recent teams devoted much of their implementation time to CPU-only
changes, synthetic wiring, repeated artifact packaging and the same small
post-prediction correction dataset. The actual search is one bounded result,
not an exclusion of symbolic learning or map-informed redesign. The raw-map
role also returned mostly locality/entropy/timing diagnostics. A new team
needs two independent accuracy-centered problem framings and a specialist
able to turn a supported question into an executable search early.

Several proposed explanations exceeded their evidence. Oracle headroom does
not establish learnability from available features. Conditional entropy on
part of the input does not prove the full function unpredictable. The v2
custom loss already rounds/abstains and uses inverse-exact values inside its
relative-error calculation; calling it simply a continuous or doubly
weighted objective is inaccurate. Loss direction, target clipping, runtime
application, population weighting and search behavior need separate checks.
The next experiment must resolve a concrete uncertainty before treating it
as the cause of the plateau.

The prior required feature groups, changed-row thresholds, shallow-model
prerequisites and0.13/0.14pp internal gates belong to specific historical
questions. They are evidence to review, not universal restrictions on a new
candidate. The hardware/contest contract and final accuracy objective remain.
The unexecuted six-run CPU mechanism diagnostic stays deferred.

Between EAT012 and this team, the existing launcher gained an explicit
hash-frozen standalone private-trainer source. Installed/private local
launch/poll/collect/stop, real C++ verification and source-change rejection
pass the routing tests. This removes the repeated installation dependency.
It verifies execution plumbing; candidate quality still needs a real search.
The original evaluator, export verifier and framework are unchanged.

EVOLVE

Phase1 runs three complementary roles. One starts from the raw architecture,
one from verified errors across the broader development sample, and one owns
objective semantics and an early executable symbolic request. Each records
an independent initial question before consuming peer proposals. They then
exchange concise measured findings and choose tests that change an accuracy
decision. Supervisor specifies evidence and resources; the roles discover
representations, algorithms and formula placement.

By minute10 publish a short READY.json and hypothesis ledger. By minute20
publish the first measured finding, private prototype or executable search
request with exact blockers. These are progress checkpoints; each role has
up to60minutes for useful research. Finish when the assigned work is complete.
Avoid repeated packaging checks or broad report expansion. One concise
report, source patch, reproducible command and necessary evidence are enough.

Every role reads exchange/brief.md at startup, before freezing a costly test,
after a peer/search result arrives and immediately before finalizing. Record
the actual consumed timestamp and SHA in READY.json. Supervisor supplies
readable copies of new remote evidence in exchange. Use those copies when
cleanroom mounts predate a result directory.

Only Supervisor invokes SSH, launches PySR and runs remote evaluations.
Each search first probes l7claude, then falls back to the main repository's
pysr_workspace. Keep at most one local search, at most2Julia compute threads,
at most3GB process-tree memory and at most3600s total job time. Fit duration
must leave time for startup, export and verification. Publish the request
early enough to consume its result within this team.

Each worker uses one compute thread and at most750MB while local PySR is
active; otherwise at most1.5GB. Check available memory before a large job;
serialize heavy compilations when available memory is below2GB. Shared
tools, installed trainers, data, task rules and evaluator remain frozen.
Use the reviewed private-trainer launch contract documented in the live
brief if an installed trainer cannot express the question. A private trainer
must be standalone, reviewed, hash-frozen and meaningfully tested.

Phase2 independently checks the strongest exact candidate and integrates
only after a preregistered r9 complete100M joint screen. A useful accuracy
gain may justify CPU cost. Require a positive paired95% lower confidence
bound and at least+0.01 composite points under the registered protocol,
alongside its declared accuracy/resource gates. Use the same output for CPU
and Acc. Local AMD performance remains diagnostic. Reuse unchanged passing
checks. The harness then performs the task's automatic dual1B evaluation.
Formal promotion, new unknown requests, five-run consistency and platform
validation retain their separate requirements.

Mixed-v1 prediction stays closed. The mixed-v0 archive has only a small
independently checked subset; preserve that scope. Re-splits of old data
remain development evidence. Independently justify new split semantics for
the actual generalization question. Avoid broad claims from one failed
representation or one short search.

## Iteration 14 / exp853-eat014-structure-research

2026-10-30T10:58:32.617645+00:00

REFLECT

EAT010-012 repeatedly retained the same algorithm. EAT013 moved research
budgets toward accuracy and executed a second real PySR fit. Its filtered
1..3ps correction search completed180iterations in473.5s, selected a
constant that changes no predictions, and failed all useful-effect gates.
Its internal lockbox remained closed. That one search is a bounded rejection.

EAT013's architecture role still spent most of its work fitting final residual
tables and a teacher over the existing estimator. Its true low-rank question
was unimplemented. The positive boundary table gave about0.00168pp on
nonforced row-weighted selection; independent acceptance reconstructed
0.0017029411pp with the original pair-population weights over all766347
selection rows. It has no complete candidate. The error role's
two-stage classifier gained0.00997pp on pair-disjoint selection, below its
declared0.10pp diagnostic gate. Positive small signals and oracle headroom
establish neither a complete algorithm nor a general ceiling.

On3,833,856 verified development rows, errors above7ps are20.07% of rows and
73.15% of unweighted accumulated loss. Label-informed bounded actions have
substantial headroom, but their realization from runtime features is unresolved.
The team again converged on post-prediction correction despite the user's
broader authorization. This is an information-flow and task-framing problem.

Objective and reporting errors also consumed time: population weight inside
tanh changed the intended loss, a replay gain had its sign reversed, and
conditional/full-population effects or improved/exact-fixed counts were mixed.
The Supervisor caught these using actual backend arithmetic and primary
results. Earlier theoretical causal claims were stronger than the experiments.
Evidence review needs to happen before expensive work, with clear ownership
and tiny discriminating checks.

The three preceding automatic scores were97.817446821,97.825547346 and
97.822631308 for EAT010-012. Their source, binary, input and full output
identities match, and sampledAcc remained98.8816287184%. Those score
differences describe repeated timing measurements. A new theory, diagnostic,
formula result and complete-program gain each need their own evidence.
EAT013 likewise retained the same output and scored97.8237712372. Across
ten identity-matched1B measurements, CPU mean153.953668s, median153.8356475s,
SD7.4754351s andCV4.85564% describe the observed protocol. All second runs
were slower; cause remains unresolved and the six-run intervention stays
deferred. The accuracy-first team design follows the repeated lack of
complete-program accuracy change.

EVOLVE

Use three Phase1 roles and one Phase2 role, each at most60minutes. Keep the
framework, evaluator, shared tools and installed trainers frozen. Roles write
private work and publish early evidence; only Phase2 may integrate shared
src/include/assets and update LEARNING.

The architecture investigator records its initial problem formulation from
raw graph/map semantics before reading prior candidate proposals or choosing
an exact-minus-current target. The symbolic investigator independently
identifies a useful fixed-architecture relation before combining peer work.
At least one Phase1 experiment must test a question whose evidence comes from
the original architecture or actual path semantics. Workers choose its
representation, target and algorithm location. No specific model, formula
location, correction support or low-rank construction is prescribed.

The third role studies the dominant measured errors and independently checks
peer semantics as soon as a request appears. It prioritizes a decisive
counterexample or measurement and executable checks over report expansion.
Share evidence through the live exchange and private READY/request files.
By minute10 publish an independent hypothesis and actual clock/deadline.
By minute20 publish a measured structural finding, small prototype or
executable PySR request. Read the live brief before costly runs and finalizing.

Supervisor alone invokes SSH, launches PySR and serializes remote evaluation.
Every new search probes l7claude first, then uses the main repository's
pysr_workspace on failure. Preserve original handles for running jobs.
At most one local search, two Julia compute threads and3GB process-tree RSS;
at most3600s total job time. Workers justify iterations and time budgets for
their question, leaving startup/export time and time to consume the result.
Use the existing hash-frozen standalone private-trainer contract when needed.
Source, config, input, objective, split, operators, selection and stop rules
must be reviewed and frozen before fitting.

Each worker uses one compute thread and at most750MB while local PySR runs,
otherwise at most1.5GB. Set numerical-library thread limits before imports.
Check actual process-tree usage. Release a large diagnostic before search
launch and publish completed partial evidence promptly. No duplicate full
outputs or artifact copies without a current consumer.

Phase2 reviews the complete candidate and requests the preregistered r9
nonrepeating100M joint screen. Require a positive paired95% lower confidence
bound and at least+0.01 composite points under the declared protocol, plus
the chosen accuracy and hard resource gates. A useful accuracy gain may
justify CPU cost. Candidate authors account for all retained work and state.
Use Acc and CPU from the same output. After phases the harness performs
automatic dual1B scoring. Reuse passing unchanged evidence and preserve
failed candidates' precise boundaries.

Existing candidate interfaces and asset layouts are changeable. If a complete
architecture prototype needs a build.py adaptation, prepare it in the private
copy with the affected callers and one meaningful build check; Supervisor
reviews the exact patch before shared integration. Shared evaluation tools,
task rules and the upstream framework remain frozen.

## Iteration 15 / exp853-eat015-large-data-transfer

2026-10-30T12:37:33.419065+00:00

REFLECT

EAT012 and EAT013 executed real symbolic searches on small historical data.
Their selected expressions failed their own declared gates; EAT013's winner
changed zero outputs. Those results reject their precise targets, features,
operators, fitting budgets and selection rules. They do not reject symbolic
relations at other algorithm sites. EAT014 also produced no complete accuracy
candidate. Its near-axis producer/trainer were repaired and run on actual
data, but the217660-cell search was intentionally not scheduled.

The recent teams repeatedly spent time on small final-prediction corrections,
rebuilding unchanged controls and packaging reports. Large supplied data and
historical structural ideas have not reached a reusable data-to-consumer
interface. Learned relations must be judged where they change the actual
algorithm, including the cost of features, retained work and output semantics.
Workers choose the specific hypotheses and algorithm sites.

EAT014 found matched-position persistence and an exact-route oracle signal.
The broader runtime pair-S4 table tested32 arms with negative effects; the
coarse hop/run descriptor preserved almost none of the exact-signature gain.
Each finding has its own feature, split, fallback and population scope.
The broader32-arm test is exploratory development evidence.

The EAT014 acceptance role finalized with stale statements even though the
Supervisor supplied a concrete source-level correction. Read
../results/pipeline/exp853-eat014-structure-research/exchange/supervisor-final-errata.json.
The0.0002623pp full1M projection divides unique selected-pair weights by
per-row weights from another dataset. Retain selected-domain0.0798775680pp;
full-population gain is unverified. The0.0012405903pp row-equal full1M
translation remains conditional on unchanged complement predictions.
Final route corrections exclude held labels; the exact route signature is
the oracle feature. Same-key persistence is a measured effect, with no
general upper-bound proof. Repair knowledge transfer before reusing it.

The last independent results retain the c1e2b03 function. EAT011-013 scores
97.825547346,97.822631308,97.823771237 describe timing variation of the same
binary/input/output. Ten matching1B observations through EAT013 have
CPUmean153.953668s, SD7.4754351s andCV4.85564%; every second invocation run
is slower. Local AMD timing and unpaired cross-round differences cannot
resolve an r9 candidate decision. Retained-output/page-cache pressure is
a hypothesis with descriptive evidence, not an established cause.

EAT014 has now completed automatic dual1B on the unchanged source. CPU is
149.128847/161.576514s, mean155.3526805s, sampledAcc98.88162871837434%,
composite97.81069730386615. Static ELF90509336B, one thread, maxRSS270225408B,
full contract, matching full output and remote cleanup passed. All four
roles completed within one hour. Twelve matching1B runs now have
CPUmean154.18683675s, median154.8250695s, SD7.28431339s, CV4.72434%.
All six invocation pairs have a slower second run. See the EAT014
supervisor-evidence-review.json; current LEARNING/theory corrections remain
assigned to this team's knowledge owner.

c1e2b03 already has positive100M and repeated complete1B evidence, but remains
outside the official exp825 package. The user explicitly authorizes and
requires official integration once1B benefit is clear. A dedicated release
owner must resolve exact evidence gaps and prepare the real deployment.
Established rules permit beneficial stage releases before the final accuracy
goal is achieved. Keep final goals visible and official-platform status open.

The EAT author's repo, original paper and public team traces support changing
roles, coordination and knowledge flow in response to concrete obstruction.
The knowledge-integrator case repairs stranded findings; the fixed-four-role
case demonstrates the need for cross-role source inspection. AMD iteration59
revisits candidates under the actual measurement regime. B200 iteration4
keeps structural investigation independent of promotion on the verified line.
These are management lessons; their GPU gains and timeboxes do not transfer
to EDA results or override this user's one-hour role limit.

EVOLVE

Use six roles in two phases, at most three concurrent workers. Each role has
at most60minutes and finishes when its concrete deliverable and required
checks are ready. Phase1 prepares data, evidence-backed algorithm questions
and the independent release path. Phase2 prepares/runs the symbolic search,
implements its consumer and independently reviews real source/artifacts.
This roster addresses this iteration's obstructions; later teams can change it.

Publish small usable artifacts early, before long reports. By minute10 give
actual start/deadline, a concise question and your first consumer interface.
By minute20 publish executable data/preparation/search/release requests or
the exact missing prerequisite. Collaborate by directly reading peer source,
schemas, tests and output, with short progress notes in your own directory.
Read the live brief at startup, before costly actions, when results arrive
and immediately before finalization. Record the consumed brief SHA and time.
Do not count an acknowledgement as verification of an implementation.

Supervisor owns SSH, reviewed PySR launch and serialized r9 operations.
Every new search probes l7claude; failure selects
/home/lhjysyx/EDA2026/pysr_workspace. Preserve original running-job handles.
One local search at a time, two Julia compute threads, process-tree RSS
at most3GB and total job at most3600s including startup/export. Workers use
one numerical thread, at most1.5GB each without local PySR, and at most750MB
each during local PySR. Stream or memory-map large data with measured memory;
release diagnostic allocations before search. Supervisor may execute a
reviewed bounded producer on r9 under the shared evaluation lock if necessary.
Do not start heavy r9 research during timed estimator runs.

Use v0 substantially for development. Audit both supplied archives, generator
and exact-label provenance, within-set uniqueness, cross-set overlap, and
role/space/distance/phase coverage before freezing model selection. Input
metadata audit may inspect v1; v1 predictions stay closed until representation,
formula, selection rules and complete source are frozen. Previously inspected
samples remain development. Do not present archive size, feature scan size or
a large reservoir as actual PySR fit exposure. Report distinct training rows,
batch draws, effective exposure, fit/selection/holdout counts and fit budget.

Fixed-architecture generalization primarily concerns new concrete requests.
Unseen role keys, spatial blocks, distance strata and architecture transfer
are different questions. Select the split that tests the proposed mechanism,
with grouped variants kept together. Do not impose historical pair-disjoint
splits or small-correction thresholds on every new hypothesis. Exact labels
may train a predictor; runtime features must be available to the executable.

Register before results the hypothesis, baseline, candidate, target/feature
semantics, split, objective, selection rule, practical-effect gate, uncertainty
method, CPU/resource budget and stop rule. New complete candidates use the
current nonrepeating100M then1B protocol. For this round, the complete100M
joint screen requires a positive paired95% lower bound and point improvement
at least0.01 composite, with a declared accuracy floor and all hard resource
gates. The CPU term projected from100M is screening evidence; full1B establishes
the stage result. Accuracy and CPU belong to the same output. Review source
and export semantics before committing costly search/evaluation resources.

The existing c1e2b03 promotion is an independent workstream. Reuse valid
matching evidence; if a decisive comparison is missing, freeze and run only
that comparison. User authorization covers main-source integration, immutable
packaging and official pointer updates after required gates. Workers prepare
the reviewed deployment in their assigned path; Supervisor executes outside
the cleanroom. Preserve every existing user change.

Shared tools, installed trainers, framework, task and evaluator stay frozen
during this round. Private standalone trainers use the reviewed hash-frozen
pipeline contract. Private build adaptations are allowed with a reviewable
patch and an affected-path build check. Keep reports concise: one question,
executed evidence, decision, exact exclusions and next consumer. Avoid repeated
checksum manifests or unchanged full control builds without live uncertainty.

The last three teams retained the same estimator. Current source reviews found role-pair/request-identity confusion, signed summary arithmetic, stale build attribution, a non-executing collision test, and a paired-sign description error. Dedicated verification is now independent of implementation, release completion and learning writes. Supervisor rechecks consequential evidence directly. Six roles, two phases, at most three concurrent and 3600 seconds per role remain.

Next-dispatch consideration: arrange source/interface verification while research authors are still active, with narrow supported findings and author-owned repairs. The prepared large-data interface can remove repeated extraction ownership from the next team, freeing capacity for algorithm alternatives. Choose the actual roster after the present final artifacts and measurements; avoid another unchanged rerun with identical responsibilities.

Replace EAT015's data/release administrative ownership with complementary
algorithm responsibilities. Phase1 pairs genuine large-data symbolic execution
with research on turning informative structural state into runtime information,
and an independent reviewer participates while both authors can repair.
Phase2 adds a fresh architectural investigation, a systems/integration owner,
and a separate final reviewer. Four roles carry algorithm deliverables; two
carry independent checking. The systems owner has useful cost/consumer work
before any symbolic result arrives. Archives and released binaries are reused.
Searches can continue across phase boundaries under the original job handle.

## Review 16 / exp853-eat016-runtime-relations

EAT016: retain hugepage-fused as the current research candidate after exact paired r9 100M and automatic dual-1B evaluation. Paired 100M saving was 0.97643675 CPU s (95% CI 0.6207869 to 1.3320866), with identical output and unchanged Acc. The 1B evaluator passed all contract/resource/consistency checks; mean CPU was 145.9028495 s, sampled Acc 98.88162871837434%, score 97.889445896. Compared with the prior c1e2b03 two-run mean 153.935905 s, the candidate is faster by point estimate; the separate runs are not a paired 1B confidence test. The >=99 Acc goal remains unmet. The 10M PySR search completed with a verified 1M-row C++ parity export, but its winner forced * 107.07862 regressed row-equal task accuracy by 0.08658 pp on selection and 0.08716 pp on confirmation, so reject integration. Fit exposure includes 10M distinct input rows and at least one full-data Hall-of-Fame evaluation; exact full-data pass count is unknown. Peak memory evidence begins after initialization, so full-job peak is not established. Team failures/timeouts and the initial collect-schema failure remain recorded; pipeline compatibility fix and retry passed.

Next action: Launch EAT017 focused on a fresh accuracy mechanism against exp825/c1e2b03, making use of the verified 200M archive while keeping mixed-v1 predictions sealed until model/split freeze. Require an early executable prototype, population-weighted and subgroup results, independent reviewer checks, then a preregistered r9 paired100M screen before exact candidate installation and dual1B. Preserve hugepage-fused as CPU candidate, enforce <=3600s per role, one PySR at a time and external memory monitoring. Deadline target 2026-10-31 12:00 Asia/Shanghai; current Acc is below 99, no promise of reaching it.

## Iteration 17 / exp853-eat017-fresh-accuracy

2026-10-30T17:20:07.145205+00:00

REFLECT

EAT014 and EAT015 supplied useful information about route and residual targets,
but no deployable accuracy candidate. EAT016 finished the paired CPU
optimization and dual-1B check, yet remained at 98.8816% Acc. Its selected
`forced * 107.07862` formula regressed by `0.08658 pp` on selection and
`0.08716 pp` on confirmation. In EAT016 only two of six roles completed
normally; three workers lost their API stream and one timed out. Large, broad
assignments delayed the first useful deliverable, and symbolic progress stopped
after one rejected formula. Prior domain findings are screening boundaries for
their exact representations, not proof that the wider algorithm space is
closed.

EVOLVE

Run three independent accuracy lines in parallel, each limited to one primary
hypothesis and a quick executable gate. A dedicated PySR specialist uses the
200M archive as a train/selection plus sealed-confirmation resource, examines
multiple plausible runtime insertion points, and launches no search until the
Supervisor verifies its frozen request. An early reviewer checks data scope,
metric arithmetic, runtime availability, leakage and source identity while
the authors can still repair. A separate CPU role audits historical variance
and preregisters the no-regression measurement; integration and final review
remain separate. Keep r9 evaluations serialized and limit the PySR process
tree to 3 GB.

## Iteration 18 / exp853-eat018-accuracy-breakthrough

2026-10-30T19:25:39.180354+00:00

REFLECT

EAT014 showed that exact route signatures carry useful residual information, while the descriptor's availability to the production estimator and transfer to unseen requests remain unresolved. EAT015's physical-path relations remain oracle features without a demonstrated runtime predictor. EAT016's selected PySR equation lost on selection and confirmation; its route-state and residual-tree variants failed their frozen population-weighted or resource gates. EAT017 independently tested rank-3/4 PSTR, localized route/phase modeling, and a PySR multisite proposal; none produced a promotable accuracy candidate. These results reject their exact data domains, targets, representations, and resource configurations. They do not establish a ceiling for other model families or full-program redesigns.

The recurring gap is conversion from promising local structure to a runtime-available, population-weighted, repeatable whole-program gain. The CPU evidence also has substantial run-to-run variation. Candidate outputs may intentionally differ from the baseline; determinism is required within each version, and CPU comparisons must use a balanced paired protocol. EAT017's frozen-source 1B measurement is pending while this team starts; integration must wait for it and use that result as the current reference.

EVOLVE

Use three independent accuracy researchers, a PySR specialist, an early cross-check, and a CPU-protocol reviewer in parallel. Each author owns one narrow falsifiable hypothesis, publishes an executable first gate within 15 minutes, and records its precise failure boundary. The reviewers check source-linked evidence while authors can still repair it. Candidate integration then consumes only independently reviewed results and requests a serialized paired r9 screen. Final acceptance audits the exact deployed source before the harness's automatic dual-1B evaluation. Keep high-memory work bounded and do not run any r9 command from discovery roles.

Use the supplied mixed-v0 archive for development. Keep mixed-v1 predictions sealed until the feature contract, model, split, selection rule, and candidate source are frozen; then use it only as confirmation. The archives contain 200M unique nonoverlapping request rows, while full label correctness and generation provenance remain unaudited. Existing checks independently resolved 4,024 v0 rows and 126 v1 rows with zero mismatches; all other labels retain their supplied status. Do not describe the full archive as independently exact.

## Iteration 20 / exp853-eat020-transfer-research

2026-10-30T21:26:17.638068+00:00

REFLECT

EAT016 improved CPU with an equivalent source change, while EAT017 and EAT018
produced no accepted accuracy candidate. EAT018's three independent researchers
still converged on related compact residual/gate probes. Multiple reports had
unit, target, biased-fit or resource errors. Independent source-linked review
caught them, but too much role time went to repeated manifests, unchanged
builds and long reports after decisive negative gates.

EAT019 repaired all 10M fit targets and ran a real bounded PySR search. Its
constant winner lost 12.593237467 pp row-equal and 12.589290215 pp
population-weighted on split 8. The result's population delta is 100x inflated;
its bootstrap interval is correctly scaled. Fit took 2,223.861 s against the
configured 1,800 s, with independently observed peak 2,731,515,904 B. The
pipeline tried C++ verification after the trainer intentionally emitted no
export. Split 9 scoring and mixed-v1 predictions remained closed. These facts
exclude the realized formula/configuration; they do not establish a ceiling
for PySR or the fixed graph's usable structure.

The observed oracle is not a deliverable. The missing evidence is a tractable
runtime representation that learns useful error structure and transfers beyond
its selection data. v0 and the evaluator's observed sample have materially
different geometry/forced-event distributions. Pair-disjoint testing addresses
unseen pair keys; the contest also requires new concrete requests on the fixed
known architecture. Choose a split to test the declared mechanism and report
these scopes separately. The remaining CPU variance requires balanced timing.

EVOLVE

Run seven roles in Phase 1 with differentiated responsibilities: distribution
support, graph/architecture discovery, empirical learning, an independent fresh
formulation, symbolic representation, numerical evidence audit and runtime
audit. Research authors choose their algorithms. They publish a one-paragraph
hypothesis and runtime consumer within 10 minutes; auditors compare proposals
early and ask authors to resolve duplication before expensive experiments.
By 20 minutes publish one executable diagnostic or a precise missing input.
Every role ends when its deliverable and checks are complete, with at most
60 minutes per role. A negative gate produces a short exclusion, not a long
packaging exercise. Preserve useful private code for the next iteration.

Use existing data producers, metric functions and evidence. Reuse passing
source/data/build checks while identities are unchanged. Every new reported
delta must agree numerically with candidate Acc minus baseline Acc. Reviewers
check actual records and consumers, not acknowledgements or generated prose.
Positive evidence must include replayable per-pair or per-row predictions.
Integration is a separate owner; acceptance is independent of integration.

Available local memory is about 6 GB at dispatch. Each of the three algorithm
researchers has one numerical thread and 850 MB working RSS; distribution and
symbolic preparation have 500 MB each; each reviewer has 350 MB. These limits
are research allocations, distinct from the 2 GB executable constraint.
Ask the Supervisor through a RESOURCE_REQUEST.json for measured larger work.
No role launches Julia or a large numerical job implicitly. A new PySR search
uses remote-first probing, at least 10M distinct positive-weight fit rows when
feasible, a measured full-size preflight, 2 threads, a 3 GB process-tree cap
and at most 3600 s total job time. Local search waits until overlapping heavy
diagnostics finish; remote search may overlap within verified host resources.

v0 is development data. Previously inspected partitions remain development
even if a file calls them holdout. Keep mixed-v1 predictions sealed until the
model, all selection decisions and native source freeze; prepare a one-shot
confirmation request for Supervisor review. The 200M archives' uniqueness and
coverage audits may be reused. Label provenance is independently checked only
on the recorded subset. Use source records without claiming all supplied
labels have been independently re-solved. Broad theory work first consults
the Obsidian index and writes the required linked note for any new derivation.

Register hypothesis, baseline, data/split, target/feature semantics, task metric,
uncertainty, practical gain, resource budget and stop rule before new scores.
Any shortlist must show positive population-weighted held-out gain with a
paired 95% lower bound above zero and a credible path to the deadline target.
Do not require each complementary mechanism alone to reach 99%. Declare the
smallest useful component gain before observing results and evaluate a
necessary combination separately. Show changed-row and subgroup losses.
Use the frozen EAT018 balanced 100M CPU protocol before shared installation,
then required full 1B tests. A different candidate output SHA is expected;
same-version repeated outputs must agree. Keep CPU and Acc from the same run.

## Iteration 21 / exp853-eat021-remote-structural-research

2026-10-30T23:17:37.424104+00:00

REFLECT

EAT017, EAT018 and EAT019 established no accepted accuracy improvement.
EAT020 added independent target, distribution and consumer evidence, but its
initial algorithm probes again missed their practical gates. The actual
PDB/FastEstimator combination used an invalid upper-bound premise; it does
not exclude other graph algorithms. The absolute joint-target HGB lost
10.2780 pp on its clean known-pair development test. The rounding classifier
gained about 0.000047/0.000045 pp in two scopes, far below its useful gate.
Neither result proves a limit for all learnable representations.

EAT020's completed dual1B used the same 31-file source, ELF and output as
EAT017/018. Acc remains 98.88162871837434%; the lower CPU mean 144.2470775 s
does not isolate an algorithm improvement. Both resource/contract runs passed.
Two roles timed out; the evaluation succeeded. The scoped 25-file research
commit 5490bf1 preserves useful implementation while shared source stays fixed.

EAT020's late symbolic diagnostics found row-equal gains of +0.019730 and
+0.025245 pp using two HGB capacities, each fitted on 133,160 rows with
66,579 selector and 66,579 held-out rows in an exposed v0 development band.
The larger learner changed 2,201 held-out rows. Those gains miss its
registered +0.05 pp gate, while population-weighted gain, grouped uncertainty,
native cost and large-data transfer remain unmeasured. Preserve them as
positive, limited-scope findings. They do not establish an upper bound for
symbolic models. The 12M census was distinct from that smaller model fit.
The symbolic author and metric reviewer reached their one-hour limits while
repeatedly refreshing package metadata; late metric-review prose/identities
remain stale and must be reconciled from its final session report.

Team process consumed much of the hour on repeated local memory-shrink runs,
formatting, checksums and unchanged builds. Researchers often tested one
small representation, then spent the rest of the budget reporting it.
Symbolic research confused source fields, later selected thresholds on its
reported test partition, and did not run PySR in EAT020. Independent review
caught defects, sometimes only after the author had finished. New proposals
need a reusable semantic fixture before expensive work and an executable
remote path while resources are available.

EAT019's true target repair was sound, but its selected constant was rejected.
Its population point delta had an extra factor of 100 and intentional
selection rejection still allowed the verifier to run with export=null.
Preserve the frozen failure. Review any successor repair and rejection-path
self-check before reuse. The EAT020 private successor, if completed, is input
to review, not automatically approved. A successful formula export still
requires a correct native consumer and complete-program evidence.

EVOLVE

Separate symbolic scientific design from symbolic implementation so a
researcher cannot spend the full hour only re-deriving interfaces. A shared
scientific-interface owner publishes a small executable semantic packet
early. A synthesis owner maintains a ranked queue of genuinely distinct
unresolved mechanisms and transfers historical findings with their exact
scope. Architecture, empirical and runtime researchers each maintain a
short live queue and move to a distinct preregistered question after a
decisive result. They stop repairing the packaging of already-rejected
methods once the negative evidence is replayable.

Two independent reviewers work alongside authors on actual producer,
consumer and measurement files. They publish short actionable findings
before long reports, reuse passed checks, and review positive results first.
The Supervisor independently verifies consequential quantities and handles
remote jobs. Integration and final acceptance remain separate phases.

Within 10 minutes, research authors publish a first hypothesis and executable
work plan; within 20 minutes, publish either a reproducible diagnostic or a
complete remote request. Check the exchange and reviewer findings after
each experiment and at least every five minutes. Carry unfinished useful
code and evidence to the next iteration. A concise negative entry is enough
to move on; reporting work must not consume the remaining research budget.

Use one concise status/report, the executable, and its actual result files
as the evidence packet. Record identities needed for replay, search freezing
or promotion. Avoid parallel copies of the same report, multiple overlapping
checksum manifests, and repeated metadata-only replays. A reviewer records
the identity and cutoff of the version inspected; an active author's edit
does not require repeating unchanged numerical work. Finalize a packet once
the author is done. Rejected methods need no release-style packaging.
The source arrays and predictions needed to replay a consequential finding
must remain available by their recorded identities.
Start from named source and final-result files. Exclude `*_events.jsonl`
and session transcripts from ordinary recursive searches; they contain
nested copies of prior commands and reports that can expand a small search
into megabytes. Read a bounded event slice only for a specific unresolved
provenance, execution or timing question. Do not spend the research budget
re-reading already summarized prior teams.

## Iteration 22 / exp853-eat022-mechanism-builder

2026-10-31T01:17:39.272868+00:00

REFLECT

EAT017,018 and020 kept the same source and Acc. Their independent scores were
97.885458766,97.888146929 and97.903243996; changing same-source CPU does not
establish algorithm gain. EAT019 repaired an invalid target but its search
selected a poor constant. EAT020's small HGB residual gains did not transfer to
a useful EAT021 large-data result: an actual10M fit completed in582.15s and
the exposed split8 gain was +0.00777485pp, below its fixed0.05pp gate. This
excludes that128-tree model/configuration, not all learned representations.

EAT021's graph bound was valid on tested rows but tiny; two corrected integer
map-action replays were negative. The remaining worker-proposed structural
questions were largely left as prose. Raw-target symbolic development spent
multiple roles on interface repair. Actual10M/1iteration PySR preflight finally
passed, with476.99s wall and3.92GB peak. A24thread/64GB full search launched on
l7 at09:05 onOctober31; its result must be read before any successor search.
Batch64 did produce a measured100M CPU saving of0.08625917s with one-sided95%
lower0.03386612s and identical output. Its1B evaluation remains separately bound.

Five first-phase roles hit their one-hour limits. Reviewers caught real faults
but often reviewed stale snapshots; authors repeatedly wrote reports and hashes
while unresolved executable work waited. The integration role spent much of
its budget finishing the symbolic producer. Team size alone does not solve
this information-flow failure. Broad labels such as architecture/empirical
also left each owner switching among unrelated tasks without a native builder.

EVOLVE

Pair focused mechanism owners with builders in the same phase. Early evidence
feeds executable work while authors continue reasoning. Separate whole-pipeline
information diagnosis, graph structure, learned representation, symbolic
consumption, complementary gain composition and runtime cost. Two reviewers
divide numerical claims from producer/consumer semantics and inspect live
versions early. A release engineer handles already-earned stage publication
independently of new accuracy work.

The first deliverable is a runnable discriminating test, not a final report.
Each owner keeps at most two live questions and moves after a decisive test.
Reviewers publish an actionable finding with file/version immediately and
check the fix once. Positive results get review priority. The integration owner
should consume tested native candidates instead of inheriting unfinished
trainer construction. Existing valid small gains may be studied in a justified
combination with a newly preregistered cost/benefit gate; avoid applying a universal
0.05pp threshold to every component or selecting combinations on confirmation.

affine action screen was negative, additive Arc tables were strongly negative,
and the route-to-expert map improved exposed fit and split-8 samples by about
0.116 pp. Split-8 had already been examined and the map's fit-only selection
history is not established, so that gain is exploratory. The map still lacks a
complete production consumer, certified fresh-selection result, primary result
and complete-program timing.

The exposed route-map point estimate would leave about 0.002 pp to 99% if it
generalized; it has not passed an independent selection gate.
EAT024's fresh-mask v2 code now passes current source-aware static review and
small adversarial fixtures, but its checksum manifest is stale and no 100M
production has run. EAT023's no-trace candidate has a two-run 1B mean 6.294 s
below batch64 with unchanged Acc; EAT025 installed its source at commit
`726d525` with local 4k parity, while complete 1M acceptance and publication
remain pending. Its 19.813 s run spread requires separate stability study. The measured
batch64 CPU reference is 146.381305 s; repeated candidate and control timing
is needed to judge stability.

EVOLVE

Keep data custody, algorithm discovery, symbolic research and CPU analysis as
separate responsibilities. Rebuild and independently audit a checksummed v0
mask packet before Main starts production or releases fresh selection labels.
Reconstruct any route map from fit-only data and freeze it before fresh
selection. Retain candidates with a preregistered, independently supported
positive joint-score gain even if the round finishes below 99%; measure each
increment toward the final threshold. Run an independent numerical review
before opening primary labels.
Give PySR a distinct runtime target and a concrete C++ consumer contract; the
main search uses at least 10 million unique v0 requests, targeting the full
100 million when measured memory and runtime allow. Record rows scanned and
used, sampling, memory and search duration. Main reviews each request and
performs the required remote-first host check before launching it.
Run the fresh-mask packet audit after packet construction, while independent
scientific and timing work proceeds. Keep mixed-v1 out of all worker mounts
for a single post-freeze confirmation. Compare accuracy candidates with both
batch64 and no-trace CPU controls under a serialized r9 protocol.

The independent audit ran the actual production verifier against the forged-rank fixture; it returned exit 0 and `status=PASS`. The real v0 archive hash and exact-member byte count match their frozen identities, but the exact-member SHA differs from the packet's producer, verifier, and source-evidence literals. Require the production verification path to establish exact source identity and actual top64 membership, not merely parity among supplied rows. Include a regression fixture that invokes the same verifier used after production and rejects a valid-source rank substitution. The independent auditor owns a binary decision and does not edit the repairer's files. Main retains ownership of remote preflight, full 100M mask production, and any later label release.

with sampled Acc 98.88162871837434%, mean CPU 138.078572 s, one-thread output
consistency, and maximum RSS 270,229,504 B. Its two CPU runs differed by

EAT028's static route map saved about 4.586 CPU seconds while losing
0.050796 Acc points, for a lower composite score than the no-trace stage.
EAT029's corrected route/context gain was only about 0.0022 pp on viewed
development data; the tested PySR expert dispatch did not clear its gain gate.
The earlier reported split-8 gain was affected by a stale base and is not
transfer evidence. These findings bound those tested variants and splits.

The fresh-mask v4 packet passed its independent source audit, then failed during
the real 100M production at its role-coverage summary. The extractor counted
port-family ids (`sf`/`tf`) where its contract requires role ids encoded by
`pair / 288` and `pair % 288`. No labels were released. Existing synthetic tests
used distinct family ids for their roles and missed this reachable case.

The main system-level constraint is the joint score: one 1B CPU second costs
about 0.00833 score points, while 0.01 pp Acc contributes 0.008 points before
any CPU change. A local accuracy gain with added lookup or dispatch work must
therefore include its complete-path runtime cost. Ablations should test both
main effects and only the interactions justified by the data.

EVOLVE

1. Add a permanent controlled-ablation/bottleneck role. It traces the actual
   request-to-output implementation, preregisters small factor tests, and
   reports accuracy, CPU work, memory, interactions, and precise failure scope.
2. Pair it with one whole-path accuracy/system researcher, one PySR runtime
   relation researcher, and one CPU-variability investigator. Keep the four
   scopes distinct and require each claim to cite frozen source/data identities.
3. Assign one worker to repair the mask role-count defect in a new copy of the
   immutable EAT029 packet. The regression must use different logical roles
   that share an `sf`/`tf` family. Do not run production or open labels.
4. After independent research, an integration steward may install only a
   candidate that passes a preregistered eligible split and resource gate; it
   also updates LEARNING.md with bounded claims. A separate auditor verifies
   the exact candidate, mask revision, experiment identities, and conclusions.
5. Keep r9 complete-program evaluation serialized under Main ownership. Any
   eligible positive candidate is frozen and reported immediately for 1B
   evaluation; no official promotion without the measured gain and contest
   gates. Main reviews any PySR request, checks l7 before each search, and uses
   `/home/lhjysyx/EDA2026/pysr_workspace/` when the remote is unavailable.

2026-10-31T16:22:19.704731+00:00

REFLECT

EAT027 and EAT029 evaluated the same no-trace implementation at scores
97.950189862 and 97.954648208. EAT029 sampled Acc was 98.88162871837434%
on 3,833,856 exact-labelled rows from the complete 1B output, with CPU
127.994848/148.162296 seconds. EAT028's static route map scored
97.948432452, and EAT029 traced the earlier large split gains to a stale
probe baseline. These results require native-consumer parity before trusting
a development gain.

EAT030's controlled ablation found F1, removal of one early records779
prefetch, locally output-equivalent and faster on a 25M development prefix.
Its local six-block CPU difference was -0.4166667 seconds, bootstrap interval
[-0.661667,-0.155]. r9 transfer and complete 100M/1B remain unmeasured.
Other first-phase work ran concurrently, so host load limits the local
performance claim. F2 and F12 did not improve the complete program, and the
reported F12 interaction interval needs its statistic/sign checked.

EAT030's whole-path screen found 86.2922% of its development loss on errors
of at least 6 ps. Its small integer correction gained only 0.008259867 pp
under a 0.025 pp gate. The surface-book polynomial screen lost accuracy and
added arithmetic; no PySR search ran. Those results exclude the tested
representations and consumers. They do not establish a ceiling on symbolic
relations at other sites or larger algorithm replacements.

The fresh-mask logical-role repair has substantial local evidence but its
worker timed out. Independent EAT030 audit and remote production remain
separate gates. The v5 launch scripts still need actual l7 dependency and
argument checks. Several rounds have spent effort repairing data machinery;
development mechanism work and output-equivalent engineering can progress
without waiting for new accuracy labels.

EVOLVE

1. Give the permanent ablation owner a concrete transfer task: prepare an
   audited F1/control paired r9 100M protocol with aligned CPU telemetry.
   Main executes it in the shared serial slot, then immediately schedules
   complete 1B when its preregistered benefit and resource gates pass.
2. Separate evidence requirements by change. Output-equivalent engineering
   reuses accuracy by full-output identity and needs CPU/resource validation.
   Predictor changes need a frozen consumer and independent accuracy
   confirmation. Exposed data remains useful for mechanism development.
3. Give one researcher ownership of a whole-path accuracy prototype and one
   engineer ownership of an executable PySR search request, with early
   intermediate artifacts. Require exact runtime semantics and actual
   >=10M unique training rows. Do not replace symbolic search with another
   generic polynomial screen.
4. Repair only the real mask launch gaps on a new private copy, preserving the
   audited scientific partition. Main performs remote preflight/smoke and
   production; a local mock cannot establish remote readiness.
5. Combine the existing CPU observer with candidate validation. Reuse checked
   input identities, existing tests and telemetry tools. Avoid redundant
   full-archive hashing or another unchanged-baseline timing campaign.
6. The integrator consumes measured results and corrects overbroad learning
   claims. A separate auditor checks all workers and exact integration.
   Main independently reviews the raw evidence before any release.

joint-objective experiment must preserve those old raw results and stopping

## Review 32 / exp853-eat032-joint-regime-search

Retain the frozen no-trace exp825 implementation; EAT032 dual-1B score 97.9315862497, sampled Acc 98.8816287184%, mean CPU 140.846007 s, run spread 15.723308 s. The anchor ablation did not freeze; other role findings and the independent audit support no algorithm integration. The separately launched raw-relation PySR study did fit the full 10,000,000-row array, completed with six observed equations after 1,230.328 s fit wall time against a 900 s configured timeout, and passed 1,000,000-row C++ parity. Its selected formula scores 98.3399226232% on only 6,032 port pairs (10.0707454% of official pair weight), +0.01737 pp over forced100 but -0.08173 pp versus the incumbent raw-tree baseline. No full-program benefit is measured; do not promote. The corrected H-C-H2 test completed all contracts and is preregistered INCONCLUSIVE: H/C/H2 CPU 126.211/149.811/133.903 s, physical-read fractions 15.0/99.8/43.8%, and H2 misses the hot-read threshold. Next action: Start EAT033 with one private PySR consumer experiment, a separate fit/coverage/timeout auditor, a bounded controlled-ablation role, an accuracy researcher, and a data custodian. Cap every role at 60 minutes and keep Codex reasoning effort max. Freeze the formula consumer and candidate before Main opens v1; run full unseen 100M with preregistered joint-accuracy/CPU and resource gates, then run full 1B only if 100M passes. ## Iteration 33 / exp853-eat033-system

2026-10-31T20:35:32.317602+00:00

REFLECT

EAT030/031/032 did not establish an algorithm change against the current
no-trace control. EAT032's selected PySR equation was `-0.081734906 pp` below
the incumbent raw-tree result on its observed split-8 port pairs, which
represented 10.0707454% of official port-pair weight. Its native export parity
passed 1M rows, while full-program benefit was unmeasured. The actual 10M-row
fit took 1,230.328 seconds against a configured 900-second limit; completed
iterations were not reported. This bounds that target, feature set, data
coverage and run configuration. It does not bound other targets, consumers,
representations, or PySR use.

The EAT032 forced-row geometry screen recovered about 0.0003 pp globally,
roughly 160 times below its preregistered 0.05 pp gate. This excludes the
tested static correction over its 33 features and split-8 domain. The EAT031
anchor representation lost 0.0962484369 pp on an exposed 1M v0 split, and its
EAT032 q8-corrected rebuild never reached current parity or native timing.
The stale f32 ELF and parity receipt are not evidence for the q8 candidate.
These results leave a precise joint CPU/Acc question; they do not establish
that compact replacement representations cannot work.

The largest research gap is full-domain accuracy: current Acc is below the
final requirement, while local formulas and exposed subpopulations cover only
part of the official pair distribution. CPU also has a large gap to the
approximately 100-second 1B target and measurable run-to-run variation. Every
route must account for all mandatory initialization, feature, prediction,
memory and output work, and explain its measured failure boundary.

EVOLVE

1. Keep independent ownership for accuracy discovery, joint ablation, PySR
   consumer design, dataset scale/provenance, fit/coverage auditing, and CPU
   variability analysis. Require early executable handoffs so timeouts leave
   a clear state and a next action.
2. Expand beyond one-step module replacement when selecting a PySR use. Test
   whether a relation can be embedded as a deployed feature, intermediate
   transform, shared subexpression, branch rule, or whole-stage substitute.
   The worker chooses the target from the current program and evidence; the
   Supervisor does not prescribe an equation.
3. Maximize actual fit data within the one-hour job and measured remote memory
   budget. Prefer the broadest feasible unique mixed-v0 fit population up to
   100M. A 10M pilot is acceptable only with a measured scaling/time reason.
   Do not reuse the failed EAT032 target unchanged, and do not report prepared
   arrays as fit exposure.
4. Freeze each promising candidate, data identities, C++ consumer semantics,
   build/parity results, output contract, joint score rule, resource budget,
   and stop condition before Main opens the 100M screen. Main runs candidates
   serially on r9. Only candidates passing the preregistered 100M gate proceed
   to full 1B measurement.
5. Separate a PySR readiness audit from the search author and a final
   independent evidence audit from candidate construction. Preserve previous
   artifacts and state whether each negative result excludes a data domain,
   representation, consumer, search budget, or machine only.

REFLECT

EAT030/031/032 measured the same no-trace implementation; their scores
97.932313821, 97.934179137 and 97.931586250 reflect repeated timing, not three
algorithm gains. EAT032's 10M actual-fit symbolic model lost 0.081734906 pp
on its 6,032-pair development slice; that bounds its target and consumer.
EAT033 changes the target to exact-mandatory-base and supplies native
post-surface intermediates. Main verified native response comes from
surface779 in extract_raw_relation_targets.cpp:198-199, distinct from the
label-derived response_target at line 219. The new search is still unrun.

Main reproduced a blocking defect in the actual EAT033 header generator:
base and mandatory are emitted twice. The previous mock C++ fixture missed
this path. Main also made and withdrew an incorrect loss-arity claim:
accuracy_loss(prediction,target,invtruth) already has three arguments.
No loss change is justified by that withdrawn finding.

EAT033's accuracy report overgeneralized its negative screens. The actual
fit/selection pair sets are disjoint by design; their counts are 47,911 and
6,032, with 5,961 pairs in split 9. The custom GBDT stores identical left
and right predicates, so its selection predictor disagrees with training.
It fit 2M rows, not the requested 10M. Small correlations and these failed
screens do not establish a universal accuracy ceiling. These concrete
errors require executable checks and precise exclusion boundaries.

EVOLVE

1. Assign exporter repair to one engineer using a private copy, with real
   generated-code compilation and reference verification before any expensive
   fit. Retain the search hypothesis and original artifacts.
2. Independently audit the accuracy ceiling claim and correct measured
   scope, weighting and predictor semantics. Separate invalid evidence from
   valid negative results; retain viable research directions.
3. Use a second phase auditor to check both outputs against actual artifacts.
   Publish an early PySR readiness verdict without waiting for a complete
   next algorithm design. Avoid reconstructing unchanged data or environments.
4. Main stages the existing hash-bound arrays while repairs proceed. Search
   budgets are independent of role budgets; the prepared 10M pilot can test
   this changed target promptly, while a larger v0 population needs an
   explicit measured scaling plan. Keep v1 and protected labels inaccessible.

primary generalization question. Unknown-pair transfer is an additional
diagnostic. An architecture-level pair identifier is distinct from
memorizing exact endpoint request pairs. Reconsider this boundary using
the actual generator and rules, and test the resulting hypothesis.

split-8 is development; split-9 aggregate was exposed historically and
again in EAT034. Neither is sealed. mixed-v1 remains closed. The v0 100M
archive is audited development data with sampled exact-label checks;
its generator provenance is unknown. All-100M label correctness is not
proved. EAT033's 10M-to-25M linear time extrapolation is a cost estimate,
not a fit capacity ceiling. Role timeout and offline search-job budget
are separate.

EVOLVE

1. Organize three complementary research roles around data/generalization,
   whole-path representation, and independent controlled ablation.
   Workers select methods; Supervisor supplies the objective and evidence.
2. Share feature/target/unit contracts early. Require diagnostic and
   native comparisons on identical requests and first-divergence evidence.
   Baseline accuracy, formula parity and algorithm gain are separate.
3. Integrate only privately, with one owner. One final reviewer independently
   checks claims and owns LEARNING updates. Main reviews raw evidence and
   controls remote search, serialized 100M/1B evaluation and promotion.
4. Preserve local RAM and disk. At most one local data-heavy process may
   run at a time, under flock on work_eat038-local-heavy.lock. Keep its
   RSS below 3 GB and other concurrent tasks below 500 MB each. Use memmaps,
   streaming and existing files. Publish remote jobs for larger work.

The EAT034 10M x31 needed-target search completed, including local 1M-row

That is a bounded negative for that search configuration and exposed
pair-disjoint slice. No full-program gain was measured. Its observed fit
time was 1170.2 s despite a 900 s stopping request; total job was 1265 s.

EAT038 prepared a 10M/1M/1M request-disjoint repartition of the same 12M
development pool, covering all 59,904 pairs in each arm. It adds six pair
encodings. Actual new selection baseline is 98.41296833321503%, distinct
from old split8's 98.42165752939127%. Main inspected the source and found
provenance/interpretation issues needing correction before a search:
stale bucket labels after rank-based allocation, old split9 exposure,
an arbitrary index grid described as architecture geometry, and combined
feature/split/budget changes described as a single-factor comparison.
These need precise boundaries rather than deleting useful research data.

The worker's default Python lacked SymPy, but the already supplied local
venv imports SymPy 1.14.0 and NumPy 2.2.6. Main ran the actual exporter
regression from a private snapshot: it fails at Rational(1,1000), which
the exporter rejects. Determine whether the fixture or reachable export
contract needs repair. Source-text checks did not settle this uncertainty.
Local preparation used 3.49 GB RSS, above its allocated 3 GB; reuse prepared
arrays and do larger preparation remotely. The v0 100M archive is already
on l7 with its full SHA verified, so no archive reupload is necessary.

EAT038 deletion and nearest-hold ablations are useful. They do not establish
capacity ceilings for retrained models or alternate representations.
Initial low-rank book reconstruction gives a measurable accuracy curve;
runtime reconstruction cost and footprint in actual bytes still need
measurement. A variance-explained number alone is insufficient. Several
researchers have inherited assumptions from the current implementation.
An independent graph-first investigator should check those assumptions
against real path semantics and earlier counterexamples.

4. Run these three roles sequentially while EAT038 continues, keeping at

reversed the residual correction sign, and attached short-domain ICC to
far rows. Correct loss shares on exposed split8 are approximately short
8.5%, mid22.6%, far32.7%, forced36.2%; sign-corrected pair means show

mid and far. These are same-slice oracle diagnostics and can overfit. No
capacity or irreducible-noise conclusion follows. Preserve this bounded
correction and do not reuse the invalid exclusions.

`interleaved_100m_fact` checks only repeated candidate outputs at 100M. The
release manifest records five-run 1B verification as false. The official

publisher must fail closed until all five are evidenced.

The candidate has two full 1B runs recorded with output SHA

additional full 1B runs remain pending. The 100M paired CPU test, fresh build
identity and independent native audit are separate gates. The live official
checkout remains untouched by this iteration.

## Iteration 46 / exp853-eat046-transfer-cpu-diagnosis

2026-11-01T01:56:42.747658+00:00

REFLECT

EAT041's candidate arithmetic, source, integer boundary and table structure
passed independent review. Its 1M request-disjoint v0 selection result was
98.504463% versus 98.412968% exp825 (+0.091495 pp) under the official
pair-population metric. On the same full 1B input/exact sample/evaluator as
EAT032, the candidate produced 98.885682% versus 98.881629% (+0.004053 pp).
The two accuracy sampling intervals overlap. Candidate CPU mean was 144.072036
s versus 140.846007 s (+3.226029 s), giving -0.023641 project points. Both
outputs were identical across the candidate's two runs; each run passed
contract, static ELF, one-thread, memory and cleanup checks. The development
1B sample has now been viewed and cannot be treated as a future independent
confirmation. Official five-run verification is still pending.

EAT041's pair table covers 27.495% of forced selection rows, with a
context/qx-band fallback for the rest. The paired CPU protocol packet in
EAT042 targets another candidate and only 100M timing. The prepared EAT041
PySR packet actually passes 850,252 forced rows to PySR.fit after slicing a
10M-request arm, below the user's 10M actual-fit-row objective. These facts
suggest three live questions: how the sparse correction transfers, which
mechanism contributes the gain/cost, and how to search a sufficiently broad
relationship without training on a small selected stratum. They do not prove
a cause or a general limit.

EVOLVE

1. Separate evidence reconciliation from algorithm ablation. The auditor
   independently recomputes the 1B and development deltas, while the ablation
   owner uses only already-opened v0 development arms and fresh folds within
   the existing fit arm.
2. Assign a symbolic-design role to discover multiple real insertion points
   from the current call graph. Require at least 10,000,000 unique rows to be
   passed to PySR.fit in any proposed search; a 10M source pool or prepared
   array does not count when stratum filtering reduces the fit call.
3. Assign a timing-protocol engineer to adapt the measured paired-output
   approach for the exp825/EAT041 pair at the full 1B scale. The role prepares
   and self-tests a private packet only; Main decides whether its cost is
   justified after independent review.
4. Finish with an independent cross-check of arithmetic, row counts, data
   exposure, timing design and file ownership. Main retains remote-first PySR
   preflight, all r9 runs, data release, integration and publication decisions.
