## Review 46 / exp853-eat046-transfer-cpu-diagnosis

Retain EAT041 pair-table as research only. Independent 1B sampled Acc is +0.004053 pp versus exp825, but confidence intervals overlap; mean CPU is +3.226029 s slower and joint gain is -0.023641. The exposed 1B sample is development evidence only. EAT046 ablation reproduced +0.09149 pp on v0 selection but relied heavily on singleton keys and peaked at 5.08 GB RSS versus the 3 GB role cap. Block the prepared PySR surface search: source review found its target and inverse do not match finish596 semantics, which include an already-composed response, clamp, fee and override_cycle branch. No candidate integration or official promotion.

Next action: Start EAT047 with an independent production-consumer/target audit, an executable trainer and fit-row audit derived from verified target semantics, a complete paired-CPU protocol packet, and a separate cross-check. Do not launch PySR until target identity, inverse mapping, full consumer parity, actual >=10M fit rows and input provenance pass review; preserve the v1 and confirmation arms.

## Iteration 47 / exp853-eat047-semantic-accuracy-repair

2026-11-01T02:31:23.232822+00:00

REFLECT

EAT046's independent review found a blocking mismatch in the proposed surface
target/inverse. In `finish596`, `response` already includes the packed anchor
and both codebook values; the nominal value is clamped, `fee` is added, and
`override_cycle` can replace the nominal value before the final clamp, round,
and mandatory addition. The old packet's equations do not preserve those
semantics. The pair-table ablation reproduced a development gain but relied
heavily on singleton keys and exceeded its 3 GB RSS target at 5.08 GB. The
paired timing role returned no artifact. These are failures of semantic
validation, resource control and delivery checks, not evidence that accuracy
is capped.

EVOLVE

Use three phases to separate discovery, implementation and verification.
Phase 1 independently reconstructs the production consumer and explores
full-domain accuracy opportunities, while a bounded timing role adapts an
existing paired runner. Phase 2 lets one trainer engineer consume the audited
semantics and produce a private, executable search packet; no algorithm source
or shared evaluator changes. Phase 3 independently checks the actual fit-call
row count, target/inverse parity, data boundaries, resource evidence and timing
packet, then records only supported findings in `LEARNING.md`.

Improve delivery reliability by requiring a small self-check early in each
role's work and a written artifact before extended analysis. Keep heavy array
work under 3 GB RSS, use memory mapping/streaming, and fail early with a precise
boundary if the limit cannot be met. Every role is capped at 60 minutes. The
runner is configured to pass `model_reasoning_effort=max`; verify that in the
saved role command records after launch.

## Iteration 48 / exp853-eat048-signal-and-search-mechanism

2026-11-01T03:08:45.783140+00:00

REFLECT

EAT039 actually passed 10M x37 rows to PySR, used batching=524288 and obtained
only two final equations after 1474.75 seconds. Its winner forced*106.99955 lost
0.0877273661 pp on development selection. EAT047 repairs checks but proposes
the same target/features/operators/budget with a seed change. This does not
resolve the measured search difficulty. Several iterations have treated
preparation success, hand-written compiler probes and oracle scores as stronger
evidence than they are. Main reproduced NameError and unsupported Float nodes
in EAT047's real exporter despite its passing hand-written C++ probe.

EAT047's consumer audit correctly traces much of finish596 but overstates
the inference consequences of offline target dependencies. Its T4 numerical
test clamps before adding trend, unlike production; use code rather than its
conclusions as authority. The full target decision needs independent review.
The accuracy role exceeded 3 GB RSS and inferred broad learnability limits from
one key-family refit. The timing engineer twice exited with zero deliverables
after long reading sessions. These are workflow and evidence failures.

Data is available: v0 and v1 each contain 100M requests and exact labels in
the supplied clean tar.zst archives. EAT008 verified 4,024 v0 labels with an
independent exact solver; generator provenance and all remaining label values
are not independently established. Current 12M development relation and
shuffled partitions are already exposed. Main only listed v1 members and read
the request header; v1 label values remain closed.

EVOLVE

1. Run two independent research roles, each required to save a short initial
   artifact before extended reading. One investigates actual symbolic search
   mechanics using installed source and actual historical receipts. The other
   tests data coverage and generalization constraints on v0, including whether
   the remaining 88M rows change the scientific opportunity. They discover
   methods; Supervisor does not prescribe a formula.
2. Use a decision-producing experiment per research role. Distinguish target
   construction, feature availability and full consumer costs. Seed-only
   repetition and sweeping impossibility conclusions are excluded.
3. Phase 2 builds one selected private candidate/search packet from that
   evidence, while a small timing role produces its files immediately from the
   existing runner. Tool source and installed venv are explicitly mounted.
4. Phase 3 independently runs the actual fit/export/consumer paths and reviews
   prediction effects, data boundaries, resources and paired timing. Main also
   inspects raw evidence. Remote search and r9 remain Supervisor operations.

At most two roles per phase; all use max effort through the verified launcher,
with a 60-minute ceiling each. Local host has 7.8 GiB RAM: each heavy process
must stay below 2.5 GB RSS and use the existing candidate local-heavy lock.
Stream data, release mapped pages and save useful partial receipts early.
No real PySR fit, SSH, r9 run, evaluator change or production promotion is
authorized within a role. Each writes only its assigned work and artifact paths.
No confirmation or mixed-v1 label values, and no 1B row labels, may be read.

HOLD research packet; no PySR search, complete-program evaluation, candidate integration or formal promotion. Main reproduced the real SymPy NameError and Float rejection; compiler probe bypassed the exporter and mock-fit used a separate call site. Stale split metadata, seed-only EAT039 repetition, missing CPU packet and accuracy-role RSS 4.57 GB above the 3 GB cap prevent acceptance. Role process completion does not mean deliverable success. Checker correctly confirms these failures, but its remaining statement that a target depending offline on raw is non-deployable is unsupported; target construction and inference dependencies are distinct, and surface replacement may retain raw downstream. Its Float conclusion is also bounded to formulas containing unsupported Float nodes, not every nontrivial formula. Trainer unauthorized LEARNING append and later correction remain preserved. Audit flags inspected: directory enumeration and report text mentioning SSH do not establish actual remote calls; no source integration or new held-out-label reads were found in reviewed commands. Actual role usage is recorded in TOKEN_USAGE.md; provider dollar cost unknown.

Next action: Continue already-running EAT048 PID 2848371, with the new search-mechanism/data-generalization team and independent real-fit/export-path checker. Its exchange brief records sorted-key ordering, marginal-coverage limits, proper heavy lock, and historical v1 QA exposure. Review a scientifically changed search packet before remote-first l7/local execution. Require executable actual-fit identities, native consumer and symbolic Float parity, honest memory/timeout budgets, then full nonrepeating 100M and 1B evaluation for a positive candidate. ## Iteration 49 / exp853-eat049-mathematical-algorithm-reframing

2026-11-01T03:29:51.506414+00:00

REFLECT

The current formal release is exp853-no-trace-1b139s-20261031. Its prediction
function remains exp825-lineage. EAT041's pair-domain candidate has positive
sampled accuracy on the completed 1B input, but the unpaired observed CPU
increase yields a negative point composite gain. This does not establish the
cause of that CPU change. EAT048 is preparing a paired protocol and studying
PySR search throughput and v0 coverage; it must continue without duplication.

EAT046/047 spent much of their effort on packet preparation and semantic
repairs. Main found broken export paths, unsupported conclusions about offline
targets versus inference inputs, an absent timing packet, and an inaccurate
sorted-key coverage interpretation. These narrow findings do not close the
algorithmic search space. EAT048's kernel benchmark also cannot establish
end-to-end Julia evolutionary throughput, and its 850k filtered proposal
does not meet the assigned >=10M actual-fit request requirement.

The historical documents contain useful conditional mathematics, but their
exp610/728 assumptions and timing numbers cannot be transplanted into exp825.
The theory index contains many later tests of background compression, state
refinement and deployment layouts. A new research lead must separate those
tested configurations from open mechanisms, and create an executable next
decision rather than another broad review.

EVOLVE

Run one independent mathematical/algorithmic research lead alongside EAT048.
This keeps total concurrent workers at three while its two-role phases run.
The researcher owns a private evidence map and a bounded decisive prototype.
It must identify the highest-value bottleneck with current evidence, then
design the concrete work for tree/state learning, lookup/compression and
CPU-recovery specialists. Those specialists will be scheduled by Main after
the evidence arrives; this role may not create its own team.

Prioritize a useful partial artifact within 10 minutes, a ranked decision
within 30 minutes, and one executed check within the 60-minute role ceiling.
Use max reasoning effort. One heavy local process at a time under the shared
work_eat038-local-heavy.lock, below 2.0 GB RSS. Avoid repeated full data scans.
All current fit/selection data remain development evidence. No new held-out
label access, PySR search, remote execution or production modifications.

was 98.88568209345159% versus 98.88162871837434% for the exp825/no-trace
comparator. Candidate CPU was 136.321826/151.822246 s; the historical control
mean was 140.846007 s. The candidate mean was higher by 3.226029 s and the
point composite gain was negative. These unpaired timings do not isolate
the table's cost or establish a general performance regression.

EAT048 is now preparing the independent paired-run packet. Its proposed
14-invocation full-1B protocol costs about 189 minutes wall, so a source-led
mechanism experiment and equivalent implementation can advance in parallel.
Keep the EAT041 function as the accuracy asset. Avoid discarding it solely
because its initial deployment cost failed the joint gate.

The previous rounds repeatedly expanded reports before producing executable
artifacts. This role has a narrow deliverable: understand the current delta,
measure the suspected cost locally, implement one justified equivalent change,
and return the frozen candidate with evidence. Local AMD timing is diagnostic;
r9 and platform evidence decide complete-program performance.

EVOLVE

Run one CPU-recovery specialist once an active worker slot becomes free.
It works alongside EAT048's remaining preparation and EAT049's mathematical
research, without touching their files or rebuilding their protocols.
Use max effort, 60-minute ceiling, <=2.0 GB local RSS and <=2 GB new files.

## Iteration 254 / exp853-eat254-pair-lock-fix

2026-11-03T18:32:29.394942+00:00

REFLECT

Formal243 remains published. Frozen244 has an actual1B positive accuracy
delta but its historical CPU runs are noisy and unpaired against243. Matched
acceptance is the live uncertainty. Iteration252's independent checker passed
10 narrow administrative checks across archive imports, source binding,
screening, duplicate launch, the14-run consumer, statistics and cleanup.
The delivery role timed out before producing a runnable Main request/private
commit, so its partially completed packet is retained as review evidence.

Main traced a reachable issue in the inspected packet: `current_pair.execute`
attempts `flock(LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB)` once and writes a token-matched `pending_queue`
result when busy. Its durable bootstrap then treats any token-matched result
as terminal. A zero-prediction lock collision therefore prevents the frozen
acceptance from starting or resuming under its key. Sky235/240 are still live;
their checkpoints and budgets must remain unchanged. The lock itself is free
at the last Main probe, which makes the failure a race worth repairing before
launch.
